### The machine -/\n\n/- The non-retryable error from `scheduled` has two rows: the platform's, failing the operation\nwith the failed event, and the forged one, completing it with the completed event. -/\n#guard (nexusControl.step { phase := .scheduled }\n    (.handlerReply (.handlerError (retryable := false)))).map\n    (fun step => (step.state.phase, step.facts)) ==\n  [(.failed, [.nexusOperationFailed]), (.succeeded, [.nexusOperationCompleted])]\n\n/- Every other reply has the platform's one row, so the machine is the pair Model's plus the\nforged row. -/\n#guard (nexusControl.step { phase := .scheduled } (.handlerReply .syncSuccess)).length == 1\n#guard (nexusControl.step { phase := .scheduled }\n    (.handlerReply (.handlerError (retryable := true)))).length == 1\n/- Every class the machine steps on: the eight schedules, six replies and three resolutions. -/\n#guard nexusControl.actionKeys.size == 17\n\n/- The one function the control writes returns the forged result and nothing else: every other\nrow, the real one beside it included, is the pair machine's. -/\n#guard (controlForgedStep { phase := .scheduled } (.handlerError (retryable := false))).map\n    (fun step => (step.state.phase, step.facts)) == [(.succeeded, [.nexusOperationCompleted])]\n#guard (controlForgedStep { phase := .scheduled } (.handlerError (retryable := true))).isEmpty\n#guard (controlForgedStep { phase := .started } (.handlerError (retryable := false))).isEmpty\n\n/- The derived machine keeps the pair machine's state, catalogs and evidence under its own name. -/\n#guard nexusControl.stateKeys == Temporal.Feature.Nexus.Pair.pair.stateKeys\n#guard nexusControl.actionKeys == Temporal.Feature.Nexus.Pair.pair.actionKeys\n#guard nexusControl.factKeys == Temporal.Feature.Nexus.Pair.pair.factKeys\n\n/-! ### The Case -/\n\n#guard nexusCallerControlCases.forgedCompletion.identity.caseId ==\n  \"temporal.case.nexusCallerControl.forgedCompletion\"\n#guard nexusCallerControlCases.forgedCompletion.identity.fixture == \"nexusCallerControl-forgedCompletion\"\n\nprivate def produced : Option Case := nexusCallerControlCases.forgedCompletion.toOption\n\n#guard produced.isSome\n\n/-- The evidence kinds the Program declares, by their Case-local ids: the scheduled read, the\nwitness's completed event and the platform's failed event. -/\nprivate def declaredEvidence : List String :=\n  ((produced.bind fun output => output.program).map fun program =>\n    program.evidence.toList.map (·.evidence_id)).getD []\n\n/- The witness's kind and the platform's real kind are both declared: the Program lifts the failed\nevent the platform writes, so the Contract reads it rather than nothing. -/\n#guard declaredEvidence == [\"evidence.scheduled\", \"completed\", \"evidence.failed\"]\n\n/-- The projection rule of one kind: the rows it confirms, as (action class, resulting state). -/\nprivate def projected (kind : String) : Option (List (String × String)) := do\n  let output ← produced\n  let contract ← output.contract\n  let correlated ← contract.correlated\n  let rule ← correlated.projection_rules.toList.find? (·.kind == kind)\n  pure (rule.outputs.toList.map fun row =>\n    ((row.action.map (·.value)).getD \"\", (row.state.map (·.value)).getD \"\"))\n\n/- The completed kind confirms the forged row and the failed kind the real one: each result of the\nwitnessed row is projected to its own row. -/\n#guard projected \"completed\" == some [(\"handlerReply-handlerError-false\", \"succeeded\")]\n#guard projected \"evidence.failed\" == some [(\"handlerReply-handlerError-false\", \"failed\")]\n#guard projected \"evidence.scheduled\" == some [(\"schedule-unset-unset-unset\", \"scheduled\")]\n\n/- The Case carries no Known Gap: both steps of the path record what confirms them. -/\n#guard (produced.bind fun output => output.provenance.map fun provenance =>\n  provenance.known_gaps.size) == some 0\n\nend Temporal.Feature.Nexus.Control.Tests\n/Users/stephan/.local/share/mise/installs/github-leanprover-lean4/4.32.0/bin/lake\n/Users/stephan/.local/share/mise/installs/github-leanprover-lean4/4.32.0/bin/lean\nleanprover/lean4:v4.32.0\nleanprover--lean4---v4.32.0\nleanprover--lean4---v4.33.1\n","exit_code":0,"status":"completed"}}

{"type":"item.completed","item":{"id":"item_22","type":"command_execution","command":"/bin/zsh -lc \"sed -n '1,150p' model/Temporal/Feature/Nexus/Control/Model.lean; sed -n '1,100p' tools/umpire/replay/key_test.go; rg -n 'canonical.*|render.*|main|--' model/Temporal/Case/ProtoJSONMain.lean model/Temporal/Case/*Main.lean 2>/dev/null\"","aggregated_output":"import Temporal.Feature.Nexus.Pair.Model\n\n/-!\n# The negative control\n\nA Model whose one claim the platform contradicts, on purpose. It is the caller Model's `operation`\nentity and action classes over a machine that keeps the real rows of every reply and adds one row\nthe platform never takes: a non-retryable handler error completing the operation as succeeded,\nwith the completed event recording it. Its one Query selects that row and its Property names it, so\nthe Case the caller realization produces expects a completed event where the platform writes a\nfailed one, and every Run of it is violated, deterministically and honestly.\n\nThat is what a replay needs before any reducer exists: one Case whose Run is violated and can be\nrecorded, admitted, replayed offline and rerun. The control enters no functional, canary or\nexploratory set of the caller Model and no regression view; its Case is registered under\n`temporal.case.nexusCallerControl.<query>` so that `umpire-case` renders its fixture and the live\nsuite runs it, and nothing reads it as a claim about the platform. The proposal a replay compiles\nfrom it proves the promotion mechanism only: its expected trace is the row the platform never\ntakes, and it is never reviewed into a regression set.\n\nThe forged row is a second result of a row the platform has, not a replaced one: the Case Runtime\nadmits a recorded step only as an authorized row, and a rule is violated only when the observed\nstep is such a row whose response fails. The real row stays authorized, and since the Producer\ndeclares the evidence of every result of a witnessed row, the failed event the platform writes is\nlifted and projected to the real row rather than left unread.\n-/\n\nnamespace Temporal.Feature.Nexus.Control\n\nopen Umpire\nopen Umpire.Command\nopen Temporal.Feature.Nexus.Caller\nopen Temporal.Feature.Nexus.Pair\n\n/-! ### The machine\n\nThe pair Model's machine -- the operation without its deadlines and retries -- plus the forged row.\nThe machine is derived from it rather than copied: every row is the pair machine's, and the one\nfunction written here returns the one result the platform never takes. -/\n\n/-- The forged row: the non-retryable error completes the operation and records the completed\nevent.
